The board (the “Board”) of directors (the “Directors”) is pleased to      董事（「董事」）會（「董事會」）欣然宣佈昊天國際建設
announce the annual results of Hao Tian International Construction        投資集團有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本
Investment Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries             集團」）截至二零一八年三月三十一日止年度（「本年
(collectively, the “Group”) for the year ended 31 March 2018 (the         度」）的年度業績。
“Year”).


Since 6 February 2017, the Company’s immediate and ultimate               自二零一七年二月六日起，本公司之直接及最終控
holding company has been changed to Hao Tian Management                   股公司分別變更為昊天實業管理（中國）有限公司（「昊
(China) Limited (“HTM China”) and Asia Link Capital Investment            天實業管理（中國）」）及亞聯創富控股有限公司（「亞
Holdings Limited (“Asia Link”) respectively. Subsequently, the            聯」）。其後，本公司之名稱已自焯陞企業控股有限公
name of the Company has been changed from Clear Lift Holdings             司更改為昊天國際建設投資集團有限公司，自二零
Limited to Hao Tian International Construction Investment Group           一七年六月一日起生效。
Limited with effect from 1 June 2017.


During the year, the Group continues to engage primarily in three         於本年度，本集團繼續主要從事三個業務分部，即 (i)
business segments, namely (i) rental of construction machinery; (ii)      建築機械租賃、(ii) 建築機械、備用零件及建築物料
trading of construction machinery, spare parts, and construction          銷售；以及 (iii) 提供機械運輸服務。
materials; and (iii) provision of machinery transportation services.

Kong are full of uncertainties and challenges. Apart from internal        戰。除內部事務及外部影響外，我們亦面臨對行業有
issues and external impacts, we are also facing elements that are         更大影響的要素。儘管多個基建項目已獲批准及開
more impacting to our industry. Although there are various                始，惟後續新資金審批遠較去年遲緩。有關資金審批
infrastructure projects being approved and commenced, new and             延遲導致項目未能跟上原定計劃，以致承建商於作
subsequent funding have been approved in a much slower pace               出建築機械投資決定時更為謹慎。
than those committed in previous years. Such delays in funding
approvals caused projects to lag behind the original schedules so
contractors tend to be more cautious when making investment
decisions on construction machinery.

MARKET REVIEW                                                           市場回顧

The Hong Kong economy remained challenging during the Year.             香港經濟於本年度仍然充滿挑戰。由於立法會拉布
Owing to a number of factors including the filibustering in the         等種種因素，本地新基建項目的審批遠遠遲於預期。
Legislative Council, approval of new infrastructure projects in the     承建商採購新建築機械愈趨審慎，因此本集團於本
territory has been granted much slower than expected. Contractors       年度之貿易業務不可避免地受到影響。然而，董事會
became more prudent with new construction machinery acquisition,        認為，建築機械之需求將於推出大型項目（如赤鱲角
and thus the Group’s trading business during the Year has been          機場三跑道系統、將軍澳 — 藍田隧道及中九龍幹線
inevitably affected. Nevertheless, the Board believes that the          等）後逐年增長，因此，本集團專注於透過引入全新
demand for construction machinery will grow in the coming years         及環保設備升級租賃機隊，從中確保向建築市場持
after the launch of major projects such as the Three-runway system,     續提供優質、可靠及安全設備。
Tseung Kwan O — Lam Tin Tunnel, Central Kowloon Route etc.
Thus, the Group focused on upgrading the rental fleet by bringing
in brand new and environmentally friendly equipment, which
ensured the continuous provision of high quality, reliable and safe
equipment to the construction market.

FINANCIAL REVIEW                                                          財務回顧

REVENUE                                                                   收入

The total revenue decreased by approximately HK$28.5 million, or          總收入由去年約 194.4 百萬港元減少約 28.5 百萬港元
14.7%, from approximately HK$194.4 million for the Previous Year          或 14.7% 至本年度約 165.9 百萬港元。有關減少主要
to approximately HK$165.9 million for the Year. Such decrease was         由於來自建築機械、零件及建築物料銷售的收入減
mainly attributable to the decrease in revenue generated from the         少。
trading of construction machinery, parts and construction materials.

fundamental in enhancing the shareholder value and safeguarding           重要。因此，本公司已採納健全之企業管治常規，當
the interests of the Shareholders and other stakeholders.                 中著重優秀之董事會、有效之內部監控、嚴謹之披露
Accordingly, the Company has adopted sound corporate                      常規以及對所有股東之透明度及問責性。
governance principles that emphasise a quality Board, effective
internal control, stringent disclosure practices and transparency and
accountability to all Shareholders.

Board for the day-to-day management and operations of the
Group. The Management is accountable to the Board for the

control of the Group and is collectively responsible for promoting        監督本集團的事務，集體負責促進本集團的成功。董
the success of the Group by directing and supervising the Group’s         事會致力於制定本集團的整體策略、審批年度發展

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                    董事會多元化政策

The Company has adopted a board diversity policy. A summary of            本公司已採納董事會多元化政策。相關董事會多元
such board diversity policy, the measureable objectives set for           化政策摘要、為執行相關董事會多元化政策而設定
implementing such board diversity policy, and the progress made           的可計量目標及為達成該等目標已取得的進展披露
towards achieving those objectives are disclosed as below.                如下。


Summary of the Board Diversity Policy                                     董事會多元化政策摘要

With a view to achieving a sustainable and balanced development,          為達致可持續的均衡發展，本公司視董事會層面日
the Company sees increasing diversity at the Board level as an            益多元化為支持其達到戰略目標及維持可持續發展
essential element in supporting the attainment of its strategic           的關鍵元素。本公司在謀劃董事會成員組合時，已從
objectives and its sustainable development. In designing the              多方面考慮董事會成員多元化，包括但不限於性別、
Board’s composition, the diversity of the Board has been                  年齡、文化及教育背景、專業經驗、技能以及知識。
considered from a number of aspects, including but not limited to         董事會所有委任均以用人唯才為原則，並在考慮人
gender, age, cultural and educational background, professional            選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事會成員多元化的裨益。
experience, skills and knowledge. All Board appointments will be
based on merit, and candidates will be considered against
objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of diversity on
the Board.


Measurable Objectives                                                     可計量目標

Selection of candidates will be based on a range of diversified           甄選人選將按一系列多元化範疇為基準，包括但不
perspectives, including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and      限於性別、年齡、文化及教育背景、專業經驗、技能
educational background, professional experience, skills and               以及知識。最終將按人選的長處及可為董事會作出
knowledge. The ultimate decision will be based on merit and               的貢獻而作決定。董事會組成將依照上市規則每年
contribution that the selected candidates will bring to the Board.        在企業管治報告披露。
The Board’s composition will be disclosed in the corporate
governance report annually in accordance with the Listing Rules.


Monitoring                                                                監察

The Nomination Committee is responsible for monitoring                    提名委員會負責監察是否達成董事會多元化政策所
achievement of the measureable objectives as set out in the board         載可計量目標。
diversity policy.

2.   APPLICATION OF NEW AND REVISED HONG                                  2.     採納新訂及經修訂香港財務報告準則
     KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING STANDARDS                                         （「香港財務報告準則」）
                                                                                           （續）
     (“HKFRSs”) (continued)

     Amendments to HKAS 7 Disclosure Initiative                                 香港會計準則第 7 號之修訂「披露計劃」

     The Group has applied these amendments for the first time in               本集團於本年度首次應用此等修訂。修訂規定
     the current year. The amendments require an entity to                      實體提供披露事項以使財務報表使用者可評估
     provide disclosures that enable users of financial statements              因融資活動而產生之負債變動，包括現金及非
     to evaluate changes in liabilities arising from financing                  現金變動。另外，修訂亦規定，倘來自有關金
     activities, including both cash and non-cash changes. In                   融資產之現金流量或未來現金流量將計入融資
     addition, the amendments also require disclosures on                       活動所得現金流量，則須就金融資產作出披露。
     financial assets if cash flows from those financial assets were,
     or future cash flows will be, included in cash flows from
     financing activities.


     Specifically, the amendments require the following to be                   具體而言，有關修訂規定須披露以下事項：(i)
     disclosed: (i) changes from financing cash flow; (ii) changes              融資現金流量變動；(ii) 因取得或失去附屬公司
     arising from obtaining or losing control of subsidiaries or                控制權或其他業務產生之變動；(iii) 外幣匯率變
     other business, (iii) the effect of changes in foreign exchange            動之影響；(iv) 公平值變動；及 (v) 其他變動。
     rates; (iv) changes in fair values; and (v) other changes.


     A reconciliation between the opening and closing balances of               附註 26 載列有關項目之期初及期末結餘之間之
     these items is provided in note 26. Consistent with the                    對賬。根據有關修訂之過渡條款，本集團尚未
     transition provisions of the amendments, the Group has not                 披露上一年度的比較資料。除附註 26 之額外披
     disclosed comparative information for the prior year. Apart                露外，應用此等修訂對綜合財務報表並無影響。
     from the additional disclosure in note 26, the application of
     these amendments has had no impact on consolidated
     financial statements.

     Customers”                                                                 入」

     HKFRS 15 was issued which establishes a single                             頒佈香港財務報告準則第 15 號旨在制定單一全
     comprehensive model for entities to use in accounting for                  面模式供實體就客戶合約所產生收入入賬。一
     revenue arising from contracts with customers. HKFRS 15 will               經生效，香港財務報告準則第 15 號將取代現時
     supersede the current revenue recognition guidance                         之收入確認指引，包括香港會計準則第18號「收
     including HKAS 18 “Revenue”, HKAS 11 “Construction                         入」、香港會計準則第 11 號「建築合約」及相關
     Contracts” and the related interpretations when it becomes                 詮釋。
     effective.


     The core principle of HKFRS 15 is that an entity should                    香港財務報告準則第 15 號之核心原則為實體確
     recognise revenue to depict the transfer of promised goods                 認收入以顯示向客戶轉讓所承諾貨品或服務，
     or services to customers in an amount that reflects the                    而款額反映實體預期就交換貨品或服務有權獲
     consideration to which the entity expects to be entitled in                得之代價。具體而言，該準則引入確認收入之
     exchange for those goods or services. Specifically, the                    五個步驟：
     st a nd a rd i n tro d u c e s a 5 -s te p a pproa ch to rev enue
     recognition:


     •       Step 1: Identify the contract(s) with a customer                   •   第一步：識別與客戶訂立之合約


     •       Step 2: Identify the performance obligations in the                •   第二步：識別合約中之履約責任
             contract


     •       Step 3: Determine the transaction price                            •   第三步：釐定交易價


     •       Step 4: Allocate the transaction price to the                      •   第四步：按合約中之履約責任分配交易
             performance obligations in the contract                                價


     •       Step 5: Recognise revenue when (or as) the entity                  •   第五步：於實體完成履約責任時（或就此）
             satisfies a performance obligation                                     確認收入


     Under HKFRS 15, an entity recognises revenue when (or as) a                根據香港財務報告準則第 15 號，實體於完成履
     performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when ‘control’ of the            約責任時（即於特定履約責任相關貨品或服務
     goods or services underlying the particular performance                    之「控制權」轉移至客戶時）
                                                                                            （或就此）確認收入。
     obligation is transferred to the customer. Far more                        香港財務報告準則第 15 號已就處理特定情況加
     prescriptive guidance has been added in HKFRS 15 to deal                   入更明確指引。此外，香港財務報告準則第 15
     with specific scenarios. Furthermore, extensive disclosures are            號要求作出詳盡披露。
     required by HKFRS 15.

     consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                    平值計算。


     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset            公平值為市場參與者間於計量日期進行的有序
     or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between          交易中，出售資產所收取或轉讓負債所支付的
     market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                 價格，而不論該價格是直接可觀察所得或使用
     whether that price is directly observable or estimated using               另一種估值技術估計。於估計資產或負債的公
     another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value of an            平值時，本集團會考慮市場參與者在計量日期
     asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the                     為該資產或負債進行定價時將會考慮的特徵。
     characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants           在該等綜合財務報表中計量及╱或披露的公平
     would take those characteristics into account when pricing                 值均在此基礎上予以釐定，但香港財務報告準
     the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for             則第 2 號「以股份支付款項」範圍內的以股份支
     measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these                            付的交易、香港會計準則第 17 號範圍內的租賃
     consolidated financial statements is determined on such a                  交易，以及與公平值類似但並非公平值的計量
     basis, except for share-based payment transactions that are                （例如香港會計準則第 2 號「存貨」中的可變現
     within the scope of HKFRS 2 “Share-based Payment”, leasing                 淨值或香港會計準則第 36 號「資產減值」中的

     measurements that have some similarities to fair value but are

     In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value                    此外，就財務報告而言，公平值計量乃按公平

     Basis of consolidation                                                       綜合基準

     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                        綜合財務報表包含本公司及本公司控制之實體
     financial statements of the Company and entities controlled                  以及其附屬公司的財務報表。當本公司達致以
     by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved                     下各項時，即屬取得控制權：
     when the Company:


     —     has power over the investee;                                           —     對投資對象擁有權力；


     —     has exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its               —     透過其參與承擔或享有投資對象可變回
           involvement with the investee; and                                           報的風險或權利；及


     —     has the ability to use its power over the investee to                  —     能夠使用對投資對象之權力影響其回報。
           affect its returns.


     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if               倘事實及情況顯示上文所列的控制權三項元素
     facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to                   其中一項或多項有所變動，則本集團重新評估
     one or more of the three elements of control listed above.                   其是否控制投資對象。

     sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits
     of the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse
     in the foreseeable future.


     The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the              遞延稅項資產的賬面值會於報告期末檢討及進
     end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that it              行扣減，直至不再可能有足夠應課稅溢利可供
     is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be              收回全部或部分資產為止。
     available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.


     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax                遞延稅項資產及負債根據於報告期末已頒佈或
     rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the                實際上已頒佈之稅率（及稅法），按清償負債或
     liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate           變現資產期間的預期適用稅率計量。
     (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively
     enacted by the end of the reporting period.


     The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects            遞延稅項負債及資產的計量反映按照本集團預
     the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                  期於報告期末收回或清償其資產及負債賬面值
     which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period,               的方式而得出的稅務後果。
     to recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and
     liabilities.


     Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss,                 即期及遞延稅項均於損益確認，惟倘其與於其
     except when they relate to items that are recognised in other              他全面收入確認或直接於權益確認的項目有關
     comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case, the             則除外，在此情況下，即期及遞延稅項亦分別
     current and deferred tax are also recognised in other                      於其他全面收入確認或直接於權益確認。
     comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively.

     Leasing                                                                    租賃

     Leases are classified as finance leases whenever the terms of              凡在租賃條款中將擁有權的絕大部分風險及回

     return on the Group’s net investment outstanding in respect
     of the leases.

     leased asset and such costs are recognised as an expense on

     The corresponding liability to the lessor is included in the               資租賃責任計入綜合財務狀況表內。
     consolidated statement of financial position as a finance lease
     obligation.

     and reduction of the lease obligation so as to achieve a                   擔，從而使負債餘額之利率固定。財務費用立

     profit or loss, unless they are directly attributable to qualifying        本化（見上文會計政策）。或然租金於產生期間

     Effective interest method                                                實際利率法

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the             實際利率法為計算債務工具的攤銷成本以及於
     amortised cost of a debt instrument and of allocating interest           相關期間分配利息收入的方法。實際利率乃為
     income over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is          在債務工具的預計年期或（如適用）較短期間內
     the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts           能夠精確地將估計未來現金收入（包括構成實
     (including all fees and points paid or received that form an             際利率整體一部分的所有已付或已收費用及點
     integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs          子、交易成本以及其他溢價或折讓）貼現至初
     and other premiums or discounts) through the expected life               步確認賬面淨值的利率。
     of the debt instrument, or, where appropriate, a shorter
     period to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.

     Interest income is recognised on an effective interest basis for         債務工具的利息收入按實際利率基準確認。
     debt instruments.

     Impairment of financial assets                                           金融資產減值

     Financial assets are assessed for indicators of impairment at            金融資產會在報告期末評定有否減值跡象。倘
     the end of each reporting period. Financial assets are                   有客觀證據顯示，初步確認後發生之一項或多
     considered to be impaired where there is objective evidence              項事件導致對估計金融資產未來現金流量造成
     that, as a result of one or more events that occurred after the          影響，即考慮金融資產減值。
     initial recognition of the financial asset, the estimated future
     cash flows of the financial assets have been affected.

     For loans and receivables, objective evidence of impairment              就貸款及應收款項而言，減值之客觀證據可能
     could include:                                                           包括：


     •    significant financial difficulty of the issuer or                   •     發行人或對手方出現重大財政困難；或
          counterparty; or

     •    breach of contract, such as default or delinquency in               •     違反合約，例如逾期或拖欠支付利息及
          interest and principal payments; or                                       本金；或


     •    it becoming probable that the borrower will enter                   •     借款人有可能面臨破產或進行財務重組。
          bankruptcy or financial re-organisation.

28. SHARE-BASED PAYMENT TRANSACTIONS                                   28. 以股份支付的交易

   Shareholders of the Company have approved and adopted a                   本公司股東於二零一五年十月二十三日批准並
   share option scheme (the “Scheme”) on 23 October 2015.                    採納購股權計劃（「該計劃」）。


   A summary of the Scheme is set out as below:                              該計劃概述如下：


   The Scheme became effective for a period of 10 years                      該計劃自二零一五年十月二十三日起生效，為
   commencing on 23 October 2015. Under the Scheme, the                      期十年。根據該計劃，本公司董事可全權酌情
   directors of the Company shall, in its absolute discretion                選出任何合資格參與者並向其作出認購本公司
   select, make an offer to any eligible participants to subscribe           股份的要約，認購價不低於下列最高者：(i) 本
   for shares of the Company at a subscription price being not               公司股份於要約日期在聯交所每日報價表所報
   less than the highest of (i) the closing price of shares of the           的收市價；或 (ii) 本公司股份於緊接要約日期前
   Company as stated in the Stock Exchange’s daily quotation                 五個營業日在聯交所每日報價表所報的平均收
   sheet on the offer date; or (ii) the average closing prices of            市價；或 (iii) 股份於要約日期的面值。授出購
   the shares of the Company as stated in the Stock Exchange’s               股權之要約可於要約授出日期後七日內接納。
   daily quotation sheets for the five business days immediately
   preceding the offer date; or (iii) the nominal value of the share
   on the offer date. The offer of a grant of options may be

32. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                               32. 資本風險管理

     The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the               本集團管理其資本以確保本集團之實體能持續
     Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                    經營，同時透過優化債務及股本結餘為股東帶
     maximising the return to shareholders through the                          來最大回報。
     optimisation of the debt and equity balance.


     The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which                本集團之資本結構由債務構成，包括借貸、來
     include the borrowings, loan from a director, obligation under             自一名董事之貸款、融資租賃責任以及本公司
     finance leases and equity attributable to owners of the                    擁有人應佔權益（包括股本及儲備）。
     Company, comprising share capital and reserves.


     The management reviews the capital structure by considering                管理層通過考慮資金成本及各類資本之相關風
     the cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of            險而檢討資本架構。因此，本集團將透過發行
     capital. In view of this, the Group will balance its overall               新股份及發行新債務或贖回現有債務達致整體
     capital structure through new share issues as well as the issue            資本架構之平衡。本集團之整體策略全年維持
     of new debt or the redemption of existing debt. The Group’s                不變。
     overall strategy remains unchanged throughout the year.
